 #include <memory>
 #include <random>
 #include <vector>
+#include <mpi.h>
+#include <omp.h>
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
 
 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
+static int g_rank = 0, g_size = 1;
+#define printf(...) do { if (g_rank == 0) std::printf(__VA_ARGS__); } while (0)
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t e, const char* what) {
+    if (e != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: CUDA %s failed: %s\n", g_rank, what,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(e));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+
+struct ParallelEnvironment {
+    ParallelEnvironment(int& argc, char**& argv) {
+        int provided = 0;
+        MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_rank);
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_size);
+        if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 4);
+        MPI_Comm local;
+        MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, g_rank,
+                            MPI_INFO_NULL, &local);
+        int localRank = 0, deviceCount = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(local, &localRank);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&local);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "enumerate devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: hybrid roomsim requires a CUDA device\n", g_rank);
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 5);
+        }
+        cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "select device");
+    }
+    ~ParallelEnvironment() { MPI_Finalize(); }
+};
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Types and Constants
 // ============================================================================
@@ -541,6 +578,77 @@
     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// Contiguous row ownership keeps MPI collectives and GPU transfers coalesced.
+static size_t rankBegin(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank) / g_size; }
+static size_t rankEnd(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) / g_size; }
+
+static void makeCounts(size_t rows, size_t width, std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displs) {
+    counts.resize(g_size); displs.resize(g_size);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_size; ++r) {
+        size_t first = rankBegin(rows, r) * width;
+        size_t count = (rankEnd(rows, r) - rankBegin(rows, r)) * width;
+        if (first > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI collective exceeds INT_MAX elements\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 3);
+        }
+        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(first);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void tauKernel(const Triangle* tris, int* tau, size_t n,
+                          size_t begin, size_t count) {
+    size_t q = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = count * n;
+    if (q >= total) return;
+    size_t i = begin + q / n, j = q % n;
+    if (i == j) { tau[q] = 0; return; }
+    float ix = (tris[i].a.x + tris[i].b.x + tris[i].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float iy = (tris[i].a.y + tris[i].b.y + tris[i].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float iz = (tris[i].a.z + tris[i].b.z + tris[i].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    float dx = ix - (tris[j].a.x + tris[j].b.x + tris[j].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float dy = iy - (tris[j].a.y + tris[j].b.y + tris[j].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float dz = iz - (tris[j].a.z + tris[j].b.z + tris[j].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[q] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(size_t t, size_t n, size_t begin, size_t count,
+                                  const float* kij, const int* tau, const float* areas,
+                                  const float* rho, const float* radE, float* radB) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[li*n+j];
+        float k = kij[li*n+j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && k > 0.0f) {
+            float b = radB[(t-static_cast<size_t>(delay))*n+j];
+            if (b > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k*areas[j], 1.0f)*b;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t*n+i] = rho[i]*sum + radE[t*n+i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(size_t n, size_t nt, size_t source,
+                               size_t begin, size_t count, const float* radB,
+                               float* distances) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float best = 0.0f; size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < nt; ++t) {
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < nt; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[(tt-t)*n+source] * radB[tt*n+i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = t; }
+    }
+    distances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestT);
+}
+

 #include <memory>
 #include <random>
 #include <vector>
+#include <mpi.h>
+#include <omp.h>
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
 
 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
+static int g_rank = 0, g_size = 1;
+#define printf(...) do { if (g_rank == 0) std::printf(__VA_ARGS__); } while (0)
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t e, const char* what) {
+    if (e != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: CUDA %s failed: %s\n", g_rank, what,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(e));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+
+struct ParallelEnvironment {
+    ParallelEnvironment(int& argc, char**& argv) {
+        int provided = 0;
+        MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_rank);
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_size);
+        if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 4);
+        MPI_Comm local;
+        MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, g_rank,
+                            MPI_INFO_NULL, &local);
+        int localRank = 0, deviceCount = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(local, &localRank);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&local);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "enumerate devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: hybrid roomsim requires a CUDA device\n", g_rank);
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 5);
+        }
+        cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "select device");
+    }
+    ~ParallelEnvironment() { MPI_Finalize(); }
+};

 // ============================================================================
 // Types and Constants
 // ============================================================================
@@ -541,6 +578,77 @@
     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// Contiguous row ownership keeps MPI collectives and GPU transfers coalesced.
+static size_t rankBegin(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank) / g_size; }
+static size_t rankEnd(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) / g_size; }
+
+static void makeCounts(size_t rows, size_t width, std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displs) {
+    counts.resize(g_size); displs.resize(g_size);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_size; ++r) {
+        size_t first = rankBegin(rows, r) * width;
+        size_t count = (rankEnd(rows, r) - rankBegin(rows, r)) * width;
+        if (first > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI collective exceeds INT_MAX elements\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 3);
+        }
+        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(first);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void tauKernel(const Triangle* tris, int* tau, size_t n,
+                          size_t begin, size_t count) {
+    size_t q = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = count * n;
+    if (q >= total) return;
+    size_t i = begin + q / n, j = q % n;
+    if (i == j) { tau[q] = 0; return; }
+    float ix = (tris[i].a.x + tris[i].b.x + tris[i].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float iy = (tris[i].a.y + tris[i].b.y + tris[i].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float iz = (tris[i].a.z + tris[i].b.z + tris[i].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    float dx = ix - (tris[j].a.x + tris[j].b.x + tris[j].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float dy = iy - (tris[j].a.y + tris[j].b.y + tris[j].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float dz = iz - (tris[j].a.z + tris[j].b.z + tris[j].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[q] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(size_t t, size_t n, size_t begin, size_t count,
+                                  const float* kij, const int* tau, const float* areas,
+                                  const float* rho, const float* radE, float* radB) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[li*n+j];
+        float k = kij[li*n+j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && k > 0.0f) {
+            float b = radB[(t-static_cast<size_t>(delay))*n+j];
+            if (b > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k*areas[j], 1.0f)*b;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t*n+i] = rho[i]*sum + radE[t*n+i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(size_t n, size_t nt, size_t source,
+                               size_t begin, size_t count, const float* radB,
+                               float* distances) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float best = 0.0f; size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < nt; ++t) {
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < nt; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[(tt-t)*n+source] * radB[tt*n+i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = t; }
+    }
+    distances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestT);
+}
+

 #include <memory>
 #include <random>
 #include <vector>
+#include <mpi.h>
+#include <omp.h>
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
 
 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
+static int g_rank = 0, g_size = 1;
+#define printf(...) do { if (g_rank == 0) std::printf(__VA_ARGS__); } while (0)
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t e, const char* what) {
+    if (e != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: CUDA %s failed: %s\n", g_rank, what,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(e));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+
+struct ParallelEnvironment {
+    ParallelEnvironment(int& argc, char**& argv) {
+        int provided = 0;
+        MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_rank);
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_size);
+        if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 4);
+        MPI_Comm local;
+        MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, g_rank,
+                            MPI_INFO_NULL, &local);
+        int localRank = 0, deviceCount = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(local, &localRank);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&local);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "enumerate devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: hybrid roomsim requires a CUDA device\n", g_rank);
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 5);
+        }
+        cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "select device");
+    }
+    ~ParallelEnvironment() { MPI_Finalize(); }
+};
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Types and Constants

@@ -541,6 +578,77 @@
     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// Contiguous row ownership keeps MPI collectives and GPU transfers coalesced.
+static size_t rankBegin(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank) / g_size; }
+static size_t rankEnd(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) / g_size; }
+
+static void makeCounts(size_t rows, size_t width, std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displs) {
+    counts.resize(g_size); displs.resize(g_size);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_size; ++r) {
+        size_t first = rankBegin(rows, r) * width;
+        size_t count = (rankEnd(rows, r) - rankBegin(rows, r)) * width;
+        if (first > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI collective exceeds INT_MAX elements\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 3);
+        }
+        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(first);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void tauKernel(const Triangle* tris, int* tau, size_t n,
+                          size_t begin, size_t count) {
+    size_t q = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = count * n;
+    if (q >= total) return;
+    size_t i = begin + q / n, j = q % n;
+    if (i == j) { tau[q] = 0; return; }
+    float ix = (tris[i].a.x + tris[i].b.x + tris[i].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float iy = (tris[i].a.y + tris[i].b.y + tris[i].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float iz = (tris[i].a.z + tris[i].b.z + tris[i].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    float dx = ix - (tris[j].a.x + tris[j].b.x + tris[j].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float dy = iy - (tris[j].a.y + tris[j].b.y + tris[j].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float dz = iz - (tris[j].a.z + tris[j].b.z + tris[j].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[q] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(size_t t, size_t n, size_t begin, size_t count,
+                                  const float* kij, const int* tau, const float* areas,
+                                  const float* rho, const float* radE, float* radB) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[li*n+j];
+        float k = kij[li*n+j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && k > 0.0f) {
+            float b = radB[(t-static_cast<size_t>(delay))*n+j];
+            if (b > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k*areas[j], 1.0f)*b;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t*n+i] = rho[i]*sum + radE[t*n+i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(size_t n, size_t nt, size_t source,
+                               size_t begin, size_t count, const float* radB,
+                               float* distances) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float best = 0.0f; size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < nt; ++t) {
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < nt; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[(tt-t)*n+source] * radB[tt*n+i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = t; }
+    }
+    distances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestT);
+}
+

+static size_t rankBegin(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank) / g_size; }
+static size_t rankEnd(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) / g_size; }
+
+static void makeCounts(size_t rows, size_t width, std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displs) {
+    counts.resize(g_size); displs.resize(g_size);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_size; ++r) {
+        size_t first = rankBegin(rows, r) * width;
+        size_t count = (rankEnd(rows, r) - rankBegin(rows, r)) * width;
+        if (first > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI collective exceeds INT_MAX elements\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 3);
+        }
+        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(first);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void tauKernel(const Triangle* tris, int* tau, size_t n,
+                          size_t begin, size_t count) {
+    size_t q = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = count * n;
+    if (q >= total) return;
+    size_t i = begin + q / n, j = q % n;
+    if (i == j) { tau[q] = 0; return; }
+    float ix = (tris[i].a.x + tris[i].b.x + tris[i].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float iy = (tris[i].a.y + tris[i].b.y + tris[i].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float iz = (tris[i].a.z + tris[i].b.z + tris[i].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    float dx = ix - (tris[j].a.x + tris[j].b.x + tris[j].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float dy = iy - (tris[j].a.y + tris[j].b.y + tris[j].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float dz = iz - (tris[j].a.z + tris[j].b.z + tris[j].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[q] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(size_t t, size_t n, size_t begin, size_t count,
+                                  const float* kij, const int* tau, const float* areas,
+                                  const float* rho, const float* radE, float* radB) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[li*n+j];
+        float k = kij[li*n+j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && k > 0.0f) {
+            float b = radB[(t-static_cast<size_t>(delay))*n+j];
+            if (b > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k*areas[j], 1.0f)*b;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t*n+i] = rho[i]*sum + radE[t*n+i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(size_t n, size_t nt, size_t source,
+                               size_t begin, size_t count, const float* radB,
+                               float* distances) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float best = 0.0f; size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < nt; ++t) {
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < nt; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[(tt-t)*n+source] * radB[tt*n+i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = t; }
+    }
+    distances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestT);
+}
+

 #include <memory>
 #include <random>
 #include <vector>
+#include <mpi.h>
+#include <omp.h>
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
 
 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
+static int g_rank = 0, g_size = 1;
+#define printf(...) do { if (g_rank == 0) std::printf(__VA_ARGS__); } while (0)
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t e, const char* what) {
+    if (e != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: CUDA %s failed: %s\n", g_rank, what,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(e));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+
+struct ParallelEnvironment {
+    ParallelEnvironment(int& argc, char**& argv) {
+        int provided = 0;
+        MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_rank);
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_size);
+        if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 4);
+        MPI_Comm local;
+        MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, g_rank,
+                            MPI_INFO_NULL, &local);
+        int localRank = 0, deviceCount = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(local, &localRank);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&local);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "enumerate devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: hybrid roomsim requires a CUDA device\n", g_rank);
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 5);
+        }
+        cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "select device");
+    }
+    ~ParallelEnvironment() { MPI_Finalize(); }
+};

 #include <memory>
 #include <random>
 #include <vector>
+#include <mpi.h>
+#include <omp.h>
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
 
 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
+static int g_rank = 0, g_size = 1;
+#define printf(...) do { if (g_rank == 0) std::printf(__VA_ARGS__); } while (0)
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t e, const char* what) {
+    if (e != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: CUDA %s failed: %s\n", g_rank, what,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(e));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+
+struct ParallelEnvironment {
+    ParallelEnvironment(int& argc, char**& argv) {
+        int provided = 0;
+        MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_rank);
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_size);
+        if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 4);
+        MPI_Comm local;
+        MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, g_rank,
+                            MPI_INFO_NULL, &local);
+        int localRank = 0, deviceCount = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(local, &localRank);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&local);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "enumerate devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: hybrid roomsim requires a CUDA device\n", g_rank);
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 5);
+        }
+        cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "select device");
+    }
+    ~ParallelEnvironment() { MPI_Finalize(); }
+};
+

 constexpr val_t PI = 3.14159265358979323846f;
 constexpr val_t ZERO = 0.0f;
-constexpr val_t ONE = 1.0f;
 constexpr val_t WAVE_SPEED = 0.5f;        // Wave propagation speed (units per timestep)
 constexpr val_t INV_WAVE_SPEED = 1.0f / WAVE_SPEED;
 constexpr int NUM_RAYS = 16;              // Number of rays for visibility sampling

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// Contiguous row ownership keeps MPI collectives and GPU transfers coalesced.
+static size_t rankBegin(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank) / g_size; }
+static size_t rankEnd(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) / g_size; }
+
+static void makeCounts(size_t rows, size_t width, std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displs) {
+    counts.resize(g_size); displs.resize(g_size);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_size; ++r) {
+        size_t first = rankBegin(rows, r) * width;
+        size_t count = (rankEnd(rows, r) - rankBegin(rows, r)) * width;
+        if (first > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI collective exceeds INT_MAX elements\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 3);
+        }
+        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(first);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void tauKernel(const Triangle* tris, int* tau, size_t n,
+                          size_t begin, size_t count) {
+    size_t q = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = count * n;
+    if (q >= total) return;
+    size_t i = begin + q / n, j = q % n;
+    if (i == j) { tau[q] = 0; return; }
+    float ix = (tris[i].a.x + tris[i].b.x + tris[i].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float iy = (tris[i].a.y + tris[i].b.y + tris[i].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float iz = (tris[i].a.z + tris[i].b.z + tris[i].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    float dx = ix - (tris[j].a.x + tris[j].b.x + tris[j].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float dy = iy - (tris[j].a.y + tris[j].b.y + tris[j].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float dz = iz - (tris[j].a.z + tris[j].b.z + tris[j].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[q] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(size_t t, size_t n, size_t begin, size_t count,
+                                  const float* kij, const int* tau, const float* areas,
+                                  const float* rho, const float* radE, float* radB) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[li*n+j];
+        float k = kij[li*n+j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && k > 0.0f) {
+            float b = radB[(t-static_cast<size_t>(delay))*n+j];
+            if (b > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k*areas[j], 1.0f)*b;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t*n+i] = rho[i]*sum + radE[t*n+i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(size_t n, size_t nt, size_t source,
+                               size_t begin, size_t count, const float* radB,
+                               float* distances) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float best = 0.0f; size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < nt; ++t) {
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < nt; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[(tt-t)*n+source] * radB[tt*n+i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = t; }
+    }
+    distances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestT);
+}
+

 #include <memory>

+static int g_rank = 0, g_size = 1;
+#define printf(...) do { if (g_rank == 0) std::printf(__VA_ARGS__); } while (0)
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t e, const char* what) {
+    if (e != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: CUDA %s failed: %s\n", g_rank, what,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(e));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+
+struct ParallelEnvironment {
+    ParallelEnvironment(int& argc, char**& argv) {
+        int provided = 0;
+        MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_rank);
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_size);
+        if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 4);
+        MPI_Comm local;
+        MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, g_rank,
+                            MPI_INFO_NULL, &local);
+        int localRank = 0, deviceCount = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(local, &localRank);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&local);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "enumerate devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: hybrid roomsim requires a CUDA device\n", g_rank);
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 5);
+        }
+        cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "select device");
+    }
+    ~ParallelEnvironment() { MPI_Finalize(); }
+};

 constexpr val_t WAVE_SPEED = 0.5f;        // Wave propagation speed (units per timestep)

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// Contiguous row ownership keeps MPI collectives and GPU transfers coalesced.
+static size_t rankBegin(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank) / g_size; }
+static size_t rankEnd(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) / g_size; }
+
+static void makeCounts(size_t rows, size_t width, std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displs) {
+    counts.resize(g_size); displs.resize(g_size);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_size; ++r) {
+        size_t first = rankBegin(rows, r) * width;
+        size_t count = (rankEnd(rows, r) - rankBegin(rows, r)) * width;
+        if (first > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI collective exceeds INT_MAX elements\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 3);
+        }
+        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(first);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void tauKernel(const Triangle* tris, int* tau, size_t n,
+                          size_t begin, size_t count) {
+    size_t q = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = count * n;
+    if (q >= total) return;
+    size_t i = begin + q / n, j = q % n;
+    if (i == j) { tau[q] = 0; return; }
+    float ix = (tris[i].a.x + tris[i].b.x + tris[i].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float iy = (tris[i].a.y + tris[i].b.y + tris[i].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float iz = (tris[i].a.z + tris[i].b.z + tris[i].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    float dx = ix - (tris[j].a.x + tris[j].b.x + tris[j].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float dy = iy - (tris[j].a.y + tris[j].b.y + tris[j].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float dz = iz - (tris[j].a.z + tris[j].b.z + tris[j].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[q] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(size_t t, size_t n, size_t begin, size_t count,
+                                  const float* kij, const int* tau, const float* areas,
+                                  const float* rho, const float* radE, float* radB) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[li*n+j];
+        float k = kij[li*n+j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && k > 0.0f) {
+            float b = radB[(t-static_cast<size_t>(delay))*n+j];
+            if (b > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k*areas[j], 1.0f)*b;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t*n+i] = rho[i]*sum + radE[t*n+i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(size_t n, size_t nt, size_t source,
+                               size_t begin, size_t count, const float* radB,
+                               float* distances) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float best = 0.0f; size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < nt; ++t) {
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < nt; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[(tt-t)*n+source] * radB[tt*n+i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = t; }
+    }
+    distances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestT);
+}
+

 #include <memory>
 #include <random>
 #include <vector>
+#include <mpi.h>
+#include <omp.h>
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
 
 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
+static int g_rank = 0, g_size = 1;
+#define printf(...) do { if (g_rank == 0) std::printf(__VA_ARGS__); } while (0)
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t e, const char* what) {
+    if (e != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: CUDA %s failed: %s\n", g_rank, what,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(e));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+
+struct ParallelEnvironment {
+    ParallelEnvironment(int& argc, char**& argv) {
+        int provided = 0;
+        MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_rank);
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_size);
+        if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 4);
+        MPI_Comm local;
+        MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, g_rank,
+                            MPI_INFO_NULL, &local);
+        int localRank = 0, deviceCount = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(local, &localRank);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&local);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "enumerate devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: hybrid roomsim requires a CUDA device\n", g_rank);
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 5);
+        }
+        cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "select device");
+    }
+    ~ParallelEnvironment() { MPI_Finalize(); }
+};

 constexpr val_t WAVE_SPEED = 0.5f;        // Wave propagation speed (units per timestep)
 constexpr val_t INV_WAVE_SPEED = 1.0f / WAVE_SPEED;

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// Contiguous row ownership keeps MPI collectives and GPU transfers coalesced.
+static size_t rankBegin(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank) / g_size; }
+static size_t rankEnd(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) / g_size; }
+
+static void makeCounts(size_t rows, size_t width, std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displs) {
+    counts.resize(g_size); displs.resize(g_size);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_size; ++r) {
+        size_t first = rankBegin(rows, r) * width;
+        size_t count = (rankEnd(rows, r) - rankBegin(rows, r)) * width;
+        if (first > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI collective exceeds INT_MAX elements\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 3);
+        }
+        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(first);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void tauKernel(const Triangle* tris, int* tau, size_t n,
+                          size_t begin, size_t count) {
+    size_t q = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = count * n;
+    if (q >= total) return;
+    size_t i = begin + q / n, j = q % n;
+    if (i == j) { tau[q] = 0; return; }
+    float ix = (tris[i].a.x + tris[i].b.x + tris[i].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float iy = (tris[i].a.y + tris[i].b.y + tris[i].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float iz = (tris[i].a.z + tris[i].b.z + tris[i].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    float dx = ix - (tris[j].a.x + tris[j].b.x + tris[j].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float dy = iy - (tris[j].a.y + tris[j].b.y + tris[j].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float dz = iz - (tris[j].a.z + tris[j].b.z + tris[j].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[q] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(size_t t, size_t n, size_t begin, size_t count,
+                                  const float* kij, const int* tau, const float* areas,
+                                  const float* rho, const float* radE, float* radB) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[li*n+j];
+        float k = kij[li*n+j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && k > 0.0f) {
+            float b = radB[(t-static_cast<size_t>(delay))*n+j];
+            if (b > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k*areas[j], 1.0f)*b;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t*n+i] = rho[i]*sum + radE[t*n+i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(size_t n, size_t nt, size_t source,
+                               size_t begin, size_t count, const float* radB,
+                               float* distances) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float best = 0.0f; size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < nt; ++t) {
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < nt; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[(tt-t)*n+source] * radB[tt*n+i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = t; }
+    }
+    distances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestT);
+}
+

codex
Implemented the unconditional hybrid MPI/OpenMP/CUDA version without adding files or changing the executable name.

Key changes:

- MPI partitions triangle rows across cluster ranks.
- OpenMP dynamically parallelizes octree-based form-factor calculations.
- CUDA accelerates time delays, wave propagation, and cross-correlation.
- MPI synchronizes radiosity after each timestep, preserving causal semantics.
- Deterministic pair-local random sampling ensures scheduling-independent results.
- GPU selection accounts for node-local MPI ranks.
- CMake now requires MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA with optimized compilation flags.

Files changed:

- [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-low_hybrid_r3/roomsim/roomsim.cpp:45)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-low_hybrid_r3/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt:1)

Verification:

- Clean CUDA/MPI/OpenMP build completed.
- Single-rank validation passed.
- Two-rank, two-thread, shared-GPU validation passed with 80 triangles and 50 timesteps.
- All 80 triangles received energy.
- Output is emitted only by MPI rank zero.
- No new source files or dependencies were introduced.

 #include <memory>
 #include <random>
 #include <vector>
+#include <mpi.h>
+#include <omp.h>
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
 
 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
+static int g_rank = 0, g_size = 1;
+#define printf(...) do { if (g_rank == 0) std::printf(__VA_ARGS__); } while (0)
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t e, const char* what) {
+    if (e != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: CUDA %s failed: %s\n", g_rank, what,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(e));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+}
+
+struct ParallelEnvironment {
+    ParallelEnvironment(int& argc, char**& argv) {
+        int provided = 0;
+        MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_rank);
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_size);
+        if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 4);
+        MPI_Comm local;
+        MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, g_rank,
+                            MPI_INFO_NULL, &local);
+        int localRank = 0, deviceCount = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(local, &localRank);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&local);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "enumerate devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "rank %d: hybrid roomsim requires a CUDA device\n", g_rank);
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 5);
+        }
+        cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount), "select device");
+    }
+    ~ParallelEnvironment() { MPI_Finalize(); }
+};
+

 constexpr val_t WAVE_SPEED = 0.5f;        // Wave propagation speed (units per timestep)
 constexpr val_t INV_WAVE_SPEED = 1.0f / WAVE_SPEED;

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// Contiguous row ownership keeps MPI collectives and GPU transfers coalesced.
+static size_t rankBegin(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank) / g_size; }
+static size_t rankEnd(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) / g_size; }
+
+static void makeCounts(size_t rows, size_t width, std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displs) {
+    counts.resize(g_size); displs.resize(g_size);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_size; ++r) {
+        size_t first = rankBegin(rows, r) * width;
+        size_t count = (rankEnd(rows, r) - rankBegin(rows, r)) * width;
+        if (first > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI collective exceeds INT_MAX elements\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 3);
+        }
+        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(first);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void tauKernel(const Triangle* tris, int* tau, size_t n,
+                          size_t begin, size_t count) {
+    size_t q = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = count * n;
+    if (q >= total) return;
+    size_t i = begin + q / n, j = q % n;
+    if (i == j) { tau[q] = 0; return; }
+    float ix = (tris[i].a.x + tris[i].b.x + tris[i].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float iy = (tris[i].a.y + tris[i].b.y + tris[i].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float iz = (tris[i].a.z + tris[i].b.z + tris[i].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    float dx = ix - (tris[j].a.x + tris[j].b.x + tris[j].c.x) / 3.0f;
+    float dy = iy - (tris[j].a.y + tris[j].b.y + tris[j].c.y) / 3.0f;
+    float dz = iz - (tris[j].a.z + tris[j].b.z + tris[j].c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[q] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(size_t t, size_t n, size_t begin, size_t count,
+                                  const float* kij, const int* tau, const float* areas,
+                                  const float* rho, const float* radE, float* radB) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[li*n+j];
+        float k = kij[li*n+j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && k > 0.0f) {
+            float b = radB[(t-static_cast<size_t>(delay))*n+j];
+            if (b > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k*areas[j], 1.0f)*b;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t*n+i] = rho[i]*sum + radE[t*n+i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(size_t n, size_t nt, size_t source,
+                               size_t begin, size_t count, const float* radB,
+                               float* distances) {
+    size_t li = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= count) return;
+    size_t i = begin + li;
+    float best = 0.0f; size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < nt; ++t) {
+        float sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < nt; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[(tt-t)*n+source] * radB[tt*n+i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = t; }
+    }
+    distances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestT);
+}
+

Implemented the unconditional hybrid MPI/OpenMP/CUDA version without adding files or changing the executable name.

Key changes:

- MPI partitions triangle rows across cluster ranks.
- OpenMP dynamically parallelizes octree-based form-factor calculations.
- CUDA accelerates time delays, wave propagation, and cross-correlation.
- MPI synchronizes radiosity after each timestep, preserving causal semantics.
- Deterministic pair-local random sampling ensures scheduling-independent results.
- GPU selection accounts for node-local MPI ranks.
- CMake now requires MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA with optimized compilation flags.
